610	    <section class="s" id="s9">
611	      <header><span class="ix">§9</span><h2>Extend the autonomy horizon as observability earns trust</h2></header>
612	      <p>How far the supervisor runs without the operator. Today it drains a cycle transactionally — one issue, one worker, one diff — and halts when it runs out of runnable work or hits a result it cannot get past. The horizon extends outward as observability earns more trust: building on unmerged work, auto-merging trusted classes, and eventually a resident process that watches its own PRs through the review-and-merge loop. That extension is shaped in <a href="ideas/drain-past-the-merge-gate.md"><code>ideas/drain-past-the-merge-gate.md</code></a>.</p>
613	      <p>The horizon is a deliberate trade, not a default: autonomy is applied selectively where the delayed feedback loop (§7) makes the loss of live intervention acceptable.</p>
614	      <p>What earns that trust is measured, not felt. <code>grade</code> reads every run-log under <code>~/.drain-cycle/runs/</code> and scores the series — a Done-rate per cycle, a trend across the last three, the <code>(final_linear_state, exit_code)</code> failures that recur, and a verdict band the operator reads against their own kill rule:</p>
615	
616	      <pre class="code-py"><code><span class="tok-kw">def</span> <span class="tok-fn">verdict</span>(cycles):                              <span class="tok-com"># cycles built from ~/.drain-cycle/runs/*.json</span>
617	    recent = [c.completion_percent <span class="tok-kw">for</span> c <span class="tok-kw">in</span> cycles[-3:]]   <span class="tok-com"># Done-rate per cycle</span>
618	    trend  = <span class="tok-fn">_trend_label</span>(recent)                 <span class="tok-com"># improving / flat / regressing</span>
619	    pct    = recent[-1]
620	    <span class="tok-kw">if</span> pct &gt;= 80:  <span class="tok-kw">return</span> <span class="tok-str">"HEALTHY"</span>               <span class="tok-com"># the horizon may widen</span>
621	    <span class="tok-kw">if</span> pct &gt;= 50:  <span class="tok-kw">return</span> <span class="tok-str">"WATCH"</span>
622	    <span class="tok-kw">return</span> <span class="tok-str">"CONCERNING"</span>                          <span class="tok-com"># pull it back</span></code></pre>
623	
624	      <p>Records (§7) feed grades; grades decide whether the horizon widens. That closed loop — drain, record, grade, then choose how far to reach next — is the delayed feedback loop paying back the live view the operator gave up.</p>
625	    </section>
626

> AGENT

Done — refinement applied to the Scale intro and §9. Verdict was **accept with notes**; all notes were Phase-4 (clarity/vocabulary), no structural rework needed. The fixes:

**Cut the over-worked "horizon" metaphor.** It appeared as five different spatial verbs (*reaches further · extends outward · widens · how far to reach next*). Now the term is **defined once** — "The autonomy horizon is how far the supervisor runs without the operator" — and the spatial language is thinned and varied after that.

**Killed the triple-repeated "observability earns trust."** Replaced with the concrete actor: **the run-logs / records earn trust** (the records are what actually earns it). Heading changed to "…as the run-logs earn trust"; act-tldr to "as its records earn trust."

**Removed two aphorisms the user flagged as weird:**
- *"What earns that trust is measured, not felt"* → "`grade` is how that trust is measured." (point-first, names the mechanism)
- *"the delayed feedback loop paying back the live view the operator gave up"* → deleted; it restated para 2's trade, so the closer is now one clean loop sentence.

**Reversed nominalizations / passives.** "autonomy is applied selectively where… makes the loss of live intervention acceptable" → "the supervisor runs unattended only where… makes it safe to stop watching live" (character-as-subject, active).

**Fixed weak stress positions.** The tldr ended on "…the work was broken down along" → now lands on "the decomposition tree."

The `verdict` code block is unchanged. Scope was the Scale act intro + §9 as asked — I left §10–§12 alone, though they share the "horizon" vocabulary if you later want the same pass run there. Still working-tree only.
